# Attribute & condition catalog (vanilla data.cdb)

Generated by `docs/gen_attribute_catalog.py` from `mod_files/data_vanilla_2026-10-28.cdb` - do not edit by hand. How to read it: [reference_cdb_attributes_traits.md](reference_cdb_attributes_traits.md).

- **used** = how many vanilla trait rows use it (a high count = proven). **impl** = the cdb's own `implemented` flag; avoid `no`.
- **Thorvald / Ginaz example**: plain text = hand-written from the house design docs ([attribute_examples.json](attribute_examples.json), edit it there); "from mechanics.json" = the Ginaz mechanics mapping; _idea:_ = generated from the id and description, a starting point to check, not a design decision; _not a fit_ = another faction's system.
- **Vanilla use** = a real vanilla row that uses it, with its val and targets: open it in the cdb and copy its shape.
- Values: `_Flat` adds the number; `_ARatio` adds a fraction (0.15 = +15%%); `_MRatio` multiplies (0.8 = -20%%, 0 = none).
- Example ids starting `Th_`/`G_` other than our units (`Th_Trooper`, `Th_Soldier`, `Th_Special`, `Th_Elite`, `Th_Frigate`, `Th_Ship`, `G_Elite` ...) are traits, abilities or missions still to be written.

Examples: attributes 120 hand-written, 7 from mechanics.json, 1565 generated; conditions 39 hand-written, 0 from mechanics.json, 213 generated.
